# RTM Master

## Purpose

Single source of truth for project requirement traceability across approved user stories, feature-owned test cases, automation layers, evidence sources, and QA coverage suites.

## Sources

- FEATURE_TRACEABILITY.md
- FEATURE_MAP.md
- FEATURE_CATALOG.md
- All approved *_USER_STORIES.md
- All approved *_TEST_CASES.md
- QA_AUTOMATION_CATALOG.md
- QA_COVERAGE_MATRIX.md
- QA_SMOKE_SUITE.md
- QA_REGRESSION_SUITE.md

## Classification Rules

- Covered: requirement has feature-owned executable, release-gate, manual governance, traceability, or approved QA-suite coverage.
- Partial: reserved for approved evidence that covers only part of a requirement.
- Missing: requirement has no mapped test, QA artifact, or evidence source.
- Clarification Required: requirement is represented only by clarification cases because executable behavior is intentionally undefined by approved sources.

| OPS-001 | `/api/v1` prefix | OPS-001-US-001 | As an API consumer, I want product APIs versioned so contracts can evolve safely. | Given a product API endpoint is called, then the request path uses `/api/v1` unless the endpoint is explicitly documented as unversioned. | OPS-001-TC-005; OPS-001-TC-009 | API, Manual, Traceability, UI E2E | Yes | Yes | No | No | Yes | QA-004 | Covered | `backend/app/main.py`; `frontend/src/lib/api.ts`; FEATURE_TRACEABILITY.md: Operations and health |

| PLACE-001 | View places list | PLACE-001-US-001 | As an authenticated user, I want to view the places list so that I can browse the catalog. | Given I have a valid session, when I open `/places`, then the frontend requests `GET /api/v1/places` and renders returned `data` rows. | PLACE-001-US-001-TC-001; PLACE-001-US-001-TC-002; PLACE-001-US-001-TC-003; PLACE-001-US-001-TC-004 | API, UI E2E | Yes | Yes | No | No | No | QA-002, QA-004 | Covered | `frontend/src/features/places/PlaceLibraryPage.tsx`; `backend/app/api/places.py`; FEATURE_TRACEABILITY.md: Places, taxonomy, filtering, sorting; Add Place To List search |
| PLACE-001 | View places list | PLACE-001-US-002 | As the system, I want guests blocked from places data so that the catalog is not anonymously exposed. | Given I have no valid session, when I open `/places` or call `GET /api/v1/places`, then the API returns `401 Unauthorized`, no catalog data is returned, and the UI does not render protected rows. | PLACE-001-US-002-TC-001; PLACE-001-US-002-TC-002; PLACE-001-US-002-TC-003; PLACE-001-US-002-TC-004 | API, Security, UI E2E | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | No | QA-002, QA-004 | Covered | `frontend/src/features/places/PlaceLibraryPage.tsx`; `backend/app/api/places.py`; FEATURE_TRACEABILITY.md: Places, taxonomy, filtering, sorting; Add Place To List search |
| PLACE-001 | View places list | PLACE-001-US-003 | As a user, I want each place row to show only scannable core data so that browsing is fast. | Given a place is returned, when the row renders, then it shows generated artwork, place name, type label, subtype label when present, `averageRating` when not null, and no fake unrated label. | PLACE-001-US-003-TC-001; PLACE-001-US-003-TC-002; PLACE-001-US-003-TC-003; PLACE-001-US-003-TC-004; PLACE-001-US-003-TC-005; PLACE-001-US-003-TC-006; PLACE-001-US-003-TC-007; PLACE-001-US-003-TC-008; +3 more | API, Accessibility, UI E2E | Yes | Yes | Yes | No | No | QA-002, QA-004 | Covered | `frontend/src/features/places/PlaceLibraryPage.tsx`; `backend/app/api/places.py`; FEATURE_TRACEABILITY.md: Places, taxonomy, filtering, sorting; Add Place To List search |
| PLACE-001 | View places list | PLACE-001-US-004 | As a user, I do not want placeholders for missing data so that rows stay clean. | Given a place has no subtype or no rating, when the row renders, then the missing field is omitted without blank punctuation, empty chips, or invented copy. | PLACE-001-US-004-TC-001; PLACE-001-US-004-TC-002; PLACE-001-US-004-TC-003 | API, UI E2E | Yes | Yes | No | No | No | QA-002, QA-004 | Covered | `frontend/src/features/places/PlaceLibraryPage.tsx`; `backend/app/api/places.py`; FEATURE_TRACEABILITY.md: Places, taxonomy, filtering, sorting; Add Place To List search |
| PLACE-001 | View places list | PLACE-001-US-005 | As an API consumer, I want list responses enveloped so that pagination is testable. | Given places are requested, when the API responds successfully, then the response is `{ data: Place[], meta: { limit, offset, total, sort } }`. | PLACE-001-US-005-TC-001; PLACE-001-US-005-TC-002; PLACE-001-US-005-TC-003; PLACE-001-US-005-TC-004; PLACE-001-US-005-TC-005; PLACE-001-US-005-TC-006 | API, Security, UI E2E | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | No | QA-002, QA-004 | Covered | `frontend/src/features/places/PlaceLibraryPage.tsx`; `backend/app/api/places.py`; FEATURE_TRACEABILITY.md: Places, taxonomy, filtering, sorting; Add Place To List search |
| PLACE-001 | View places list | PLACE-001-US-006 | As the system, I want bounded place listing so that large catalogs do not overload the client. | Given no `limit` is supplied, when `GET /api/v1/places` runs, then the API returns no more than the default bounded page and never returns an unbounded full catalog. | LIST-008-US-008-TC-001; PLACE-001-US-006-TC-001; PLACE-001-US-006-TC-002; PLACE-001-US-006-TC-003 | API, UI E2E | Yes | Yes | No | No | No | QA-002, QA-004 | Covered | `frontend/src/features/places/PlaceLibraryPage.tsx`; `backend/app/api/places.py`; FEATURE_TRACEABILITY.md: Places, taxonomy, filtering, sorting; Add Place To List search |
